## Run summary

**Quiet run — nothing to do.**

- Checkpoint: uid 8640 / 2026-09-02 08:06:08 -0700 (from a prior run)
- IMAP: connected fine, INBOX and Sent identified. INBOX's current max UID is exactly 8640 (matches checkpoint) — no new inbound mail. Sent checked by date (since Sent has its own independent, much lower UID space — checking there against 8640 directly would always show zero) — no new outbound mail after the checkpoint timestamp either.
- Tickets created: 0. Activity logged: 0.
- Stale tickets (48h+, waiting on client): 0 — no nudges drafted, no notify-pending call needed.
- Checkpoint: left unchanged, since nothing new was seen to advance it.

One note for future runs: the checkpoint scheme stores a single UID that's really INBOX's UID. Since Sent's UID numbering is separate and currently much lower (max 1210 vs. checkpoint's 8640), a plain "UID > checkpoint" filter against Sent will silently miss everything indefinitely. I worked around it this run by cross-checking Sent via the checkpoint's date instead, which is what I'd recommend doing every run rather than relying on the raw UID comparison for that folder.
